+    static Stream<Arguments> invalidPolicies() {
+        String carrier = "{\"carrier\":\"ZZ\",\"percentage\":1}";
+        return Stream.of(Arguments.of("blank name", policyJson(" ", 1, null, carrier), 40023),
+                Arguments.of("name of 51 characters", policyJson("x".repeat(51), 1, null, carrier), 40023),
+                Arguments.of("without priority", policyJson("Name", null, null, carrier), 40024),
+                Arguments.of("negative priority", policyJson("Name", -1, null, carrier), 40024),
+                Arguments.of("start equal to expire",
+                        "{\"name\":\"Name\",\"priority\":1,\"startDate\":\"2027-01-01T00:00:00\","
+                                + "\"expireDate\":\"2027-01-01T00:00:00\",\"carriers\":[" + carrier + "]}",
+                        40025),
+                Arguments.of("without dates", "{\"name\":\"Name\",\"priority\":1,\"carriers\":[" + carrier + "]}",
+                        40025),
+                Arguments.of("without carriers", policyJson("Name", 1, null), 40026),
+                Arguments.of("duplicated carriers", policyJson("Name", 1, null, carrier,
+                        "{\"carrier\":\"zz\",\"percentage\":2}"), 40026),
+                Arguments.of("carrier of three characters",
+                        policyJson("Name", 1, null, "{\"carrier\":\"ZZZ\",\"percentage\":1}"), 40027),
+                Arguments.of("percentage higher than 100",
+                        policyJson("Name", 1, null, "{\"carrier\":\"ZZ\",\"percentage\":100.01}"), 40028),
+                Arguments.of("negative percentage",
+                        policyJson("Name", 1, null, "{\"carrier\":\"ZZ\",\"percentage\":-1}"), 40028),
+                Arguments.of("without percentage", policyJson("Name", 1, null, "{\"carrier\":\"ZZ\"}"), 40028));
+    }
+
+    @ParameterizedTest(name = "[#25 #7] {0} -> {2}")
+    @MethodSource("invalidPolicies")
+    void invalidPolicy(String description, String body, int code) {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = call(HttpMethod.POST, POLICIES, body);
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);
+        JsonNode error = Json.parse(response.getBody());
+        assertThat(error.get("code").asInt()).isEqualTo(code);
+        assertThat(error.get("shortDescription").asText()).isNotBlank();
+        assertThat(error.get("reasons").isNull()).isTrue();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#25] the status is required (40029) and the carrier of the path is validated (40027)")
+    void invalidStatusAndCarrier() {
+        ResponseEntity<String> status = call(HttpMethod.PATCH, POLICIES + "/1/status", "{}");
+        ResponseEntity<String> carrier = call(HttpMethod.PUT, POLICIES + "/1/carriers/ARG", "{\"percentage\":1}");
+        ResponseEntity<String> percentage = call(HttpMethod.PUT, POLICIES + "/1/carriers/AR", "{\"percentage\":101}");
+
+        assertThat(Json.parse(status.getBody()).get("code").asInt()).isEqualTo(40029);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(carrier.getBody()).get("code").asInt()).isEqualTo(40027);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(percentage.getBody()).get("code").asInt()).isEqualTo(40028);
+    }
+
+    // private function(s)
+
+    private static String policyJson(String name, Integer priority, Boolean active, String... carriers) {
+        return "{\"name\":\"" + name + "\",\"priority\":" + priority + (active != null ? ",\"active\":" + active : "")
+                + ",\"startDate\":\"2026-01-01T00:00:00\",\"expireDate\":\"2029-01-01T00:00:00\",\"carriers\":["
+                + String.join(",", carriers) + "]}";
+    }
+
+    private long create(String body) {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = call(HttpMethod.POST, POLICIES, body);
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.CREATED);
+        long id = Json.parse(response.getBody()).get("id").asLong();
+        createdPolicies.add(id);
+        return id;
+    }
+
+    private ResponseEntity<String> call(HttpMethod method, String pathAndQuery, String body) {
+        HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();
+        headers.set("X-Api-Key", API_KEY);
+        headers.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
+
+        return restTemplate.exchange(URI.create(restTemplate.getRootUri()).resolve(CONTEXT_PATH + pathAndQuery),
+                method, new HttpEntity<>(body, headers), String.class);
+    }
+
+    private List<Long> ids(ResponseEntity<String> response) {
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        List<Long> ids = Lists.newArrayList();
+        Json.parse(response.getBody()).forEach(policy -> ids.add(policy.get("id").asLong()));
+        return ids;
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, String> percentages(JsonNode policy) {
+        Map<String, String> percentages = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
+        policy.get("carriers").forEach(carrier -> percentages.put(carrier.get("carrier").asText(),
+                decimal(carrier.get("percentage"))));
+        return percentages;
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, Long> carrierIds(long policyId) {
+        Map<String, Long> ids = new java.util.HashMap<>();
+        jdbcTemplate.query("select id, carrier from commercial_policy_carrier where commercial_policy_id = ?",
+                rs -> {
+                    ids.put(rs.getString("carrier"), rs.getLong("id"));
+                }, policyId);
+        return ids;
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.provider.connector;
+
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
+
+import java.util.List;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

+
+import com.github.tomakehurst.wiremock.verification.LoggedRequest;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.request.ClustersAvailabilityRequestDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.shared.exception.TWAException;
+import com.twa.flights.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;

+/**
+ * Characterization of the requests that the connectors send to the providers and of the limit of the amount

+    private BetaConnector betaConnector;
+
+    @Autowired
+    private GammaConnector gammaConnector;
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#23] round trip: origin, destination and departureDate of the first route, returnDate is the second date")
+    void requestOfRoundTrip() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        ClustersAvailabilityRequestDTO request = Requests.roundTrip();
+        request.setCabin(" business ");
+        request.setAmount(2);
+
+        alphaConnector.availability(request);
+        betaConnector.availability(request);
+        gammaConnector.availability(request);
+
+        String expected = "/itineraries?origin=BUE&destination=MIA&departureDate=2027-01-14&adults=1&children=1"
+                + "&infants=1&returnDate=2027-01-24&amount=2&cabin=BUSINESS";
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.ALPHA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.ALPHA + expected);
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.BETA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.BETA + expected);
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.GAMMA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.GAMMA + expected);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#23] one way: without returnDate, without amount and the cabin ECONOMY by default")
+    void oneWayRequest() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        alphaConnector.availability(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 2, 0, 0));
+
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.ALPHA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.ALPHA
+                + "/itineraries?origin=BUE&destination=MIA&departureDate=2027-01-14&adults=2&children=0&infants=0"
+                + "&cabin=ECONOMY");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#23] multiple destinations: only the first route is sent, the third one is ignored")
+    void multipleDestinationsRequest() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        betaConnector.availability(Requests.request("BUE,MIA,SCL", "MIA,SCL,BUE", "2027-01-14,2027-01-20,2027-01-24",
+                1, 0, 0));
+
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.BETA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.BETA
+                + "/itineraries?origin=BUE&destination=MIA&departureDate=2027-01-14&adults=1&children=0&infants=0"
+                + "&returnDate=2027-01-20&cabin=ECONOMY");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#23] without children or infants the parameter is sent without a value")
+    void requestWithoutChildrenAndInfants() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        gammaConnector.availability(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 1, null, null));
+
+        assertThat(onlyRequest(ProviderStubs.GAMMA).getUrl()).isEqualTo(ProviderStubs.GAMMA
+                + "/itineraries?origin=BUE&destination=MIA&departureDate=2027-01-14&adults=1&children&infants"
+                + "&cabin=ECONOMY");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#16] each connector cuts the result to the amount, without amount it returns everything")
+    void amountLimitsEachProvider() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        ClustersAvailabilityRequestDTO withAmount = Requests.roundTrip();
+        withAmount.setAmount(2);
+
+        assertThat(alphaConnector.availability(Requests.roundTrip())).hasSize(5);
+        assertThat(alphaConnector.availability(withAmount)).hasSize(2);
+        assertThat(betaConnector.availability(withAmount)).hasSize(2);
+        assertThat(gammaConnector.availability(withAmount)).hasSize(2);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#10] an error of the provider throws a TWAException in the connector")
+    void errorOfTheProvider() {
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.ALPHA, 500);
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.BETA, 404);
+
+        assertThatThrownBy(() -> alphaConnector.availability(Requests.roundTrip())).isInstanceOf(TWAException.class)
+                .hasMessage("Error while calling provider alpha endpoint");
+        assertThatThrownBy(() -> betaConnector.availability(Requests.roundTrip())).isInstanceOf(TWAException.class)
+                .hasMessage("Error while calling provider beta endpoint");
+    }
+
+    private LoggedRequest onlyRequest(String provider) {
+        List<LoggedRequest> requests = providers.requests(provider);
+        assertThat(requests).hasSize(1);
+        return requests.get(0);
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644

+package com.twa.flights.provider.helper;
+
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+
+import com.twa.flights.shared.exception.TWAException;
+
+/**
+ * Characterization of the calculation of the duration with the time zones (behavior #15). Only the methods that do
+ * not use the catalog, the calculation with the cities of the database is in ProvidersSearchTest.
+ */
+class FlightHelperTest {
+
+    private static final String BUENOS_AIRES = "America/Argentina/Buenos_Aires";
+    private static final String NEW_YORK = "America/New_York";
+
+    // The catalog is not used by these methods
+    private final FlightHelper helper = new FlightHelper(null);
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] the duration uses the time zone of each city (BUE 04:45 to MIA 23:45 is 21:00)")
+    void durationWithTimeZones() {
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-14", "04:45", BUENOS_AIRES, "2027-01-14", "23:45", NEW_YORK))
+                .isEqualTo("21:00");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] without time zones the local times are compared")
+    void durationWithoutTimeZones() {
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-14", "04:45", null, "2027-01-14", "23:45", ""))
+                .isEqualTo("19:00");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] the format is HH:mm with two digits, and more than two digits after 99 hours")
+    void format() {
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-14", "10:00", null, "2027-01-14", "10:05", null))
+                .isEqualTo("00:05");
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-14", "10:00", null, "2027-01-14", "10:00", null))
+                .isEqualTo("00:00");
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-10", "00:00", null, "2027-01-14", "04:30", null))
+                .isEqualTo("100:30");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] the daylight saving time of the date changes the duration (New York, 2027-03-14)")
+    void daylightSavingTime() {
+        // Before the change New York is UTC-5 and after it UTC-4, Buenos Aires does not change (UTC-3)
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-03-13", "22:00", NEW_YORK, "2027-03-14", "10:00", BUENOS_AIRES))
+                .isEqualTo("10:00");
+        assertThat(helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-03-14", "22:00", NEW_YORK, "2027-03-15", "10:00", BUENOS_AIRES))
+                .isEqualTo("11:00");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] an arrival before the departure (with the time zones) throws an exception")
+    void arrivalBeforeDeparture() {
+        // MIA 07:15 (12:15 UTC) to SAO 09:00 (12:00 UTC), the case of the recorded response of Gamma
+        assertThatThrownBy(() -> helper.timeBetweenDateTime("2027-01-24", "07:15", NEW_YORK, "2027-01-24", "09:00",
+                "America/Sao_Paulo")).isInstanceOf(TWAException.class)
+                .hasMessage("The arrival of the flight is before the departure");
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.provider.mapper;
+
+import static com.twa.flights.support.ItineraryTestData.ids;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+
+import java.util.List;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.BaggageAllowanceDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.ItineraryDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.LegDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.provider.dto.AlphaAvailabilityResponse;
+import com.twa.flights.shared.enums.CabinClass;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.FlightType;
+import com.twa.flights.provider.api.Provider;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Fixtures;
+import com.twa.flights.support.ProviderStubs;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Requests;
+
+// Characterization of the transformation of the provider Alpha with its recorded response (behaviors #11 and #24)
+@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
+class AlphaItineraryMapperTest {
+
+    private final AlphaItineraryMapper mapper = new AlphaItineraryMapper(new ProviderMapperHelper());
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11] each offer is an itinerary and each journey is a segment")
+    void recordedResponse() {
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP));
+
+        assertThat(ids(itineraries)).containsExactly("d15896b6-d0f0-4808-a420-82ea5650421b",
+                "adb49624-bf8c-4da6-904e-2892cba9f9b6", "99f1ff5c-53dc-4a70-8784-bf8e5da5d3c9",
+                "e485161b-e5e1-44fa-a786-3aaab579c6a6", "7e211600-69f4-48d0-b068-ddf019f5b6d2");
+
+        ItineraryDTO first = itineraries.get(0);
+        assertThat(first.getCarrier()).isEqualTo("AA");
+        assertThat(first.getProvider()).isEqualTo(Provider.ALPHA);
+        assertThat(first.getFlightType()).isEqualTo(FlightType.ROUND_TRIP);
+        assertThat(first.getCabin()).isEqualTo(CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+        assertThat(first.getSegments()).hasSize(2);
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(0).getFlightDuration()).isNull();
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(0).getLegs()).containsExactly(
+                new LegDTO("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", "00:45", "2027-01-14", "20:15", 3381, null));
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(1).getLegs()).containsExactly(
+                new LegDTO("MIA", "BUE", "2027-01-24", "02:15", "2027-01-24", "23:30", 2340, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11 #21] the fares come by type ADT, CHD and INF")
+    void faresByPassengerType() {
+        ItineraryDTO first = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP)).get(0);
+
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getAdults().getSubtotal()).hasToString("561.30");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getChildren().getSubtotal()).hasToString("809.76");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getInfants().getSubtotal()).hasToString("160.21");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11 #22] the baggage comes from luggage.cabin and luggage.hold, without pieces the weight is null")
+    void baggage() {
+        ItineraryDTO first = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP)).get(0);
+
+        assertThat(first.getBaggage().getCarryOn()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 8));
+        assertThat(first.getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(0, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11] the flights of a journey are the legs of the segment, in the same order")
+    void connections() {
+        ItineraryDTO withConnection = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP)).get(3);
+
+        assertThat(withConnection.getSegments().get(0).getLegs()).extracting(LegDTO::getOrigin, LegDTO::getNumber)
+                .containsExactly(org.assertj.core.groups.Tuple.tuple("BUE", 4843),
+                        org.assertj.core.groups.Tuple.tuple("SAO", 361));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11] the cabin uses the booking classes Y, W and C")
+    void cabinWithBookingClasses() {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        offer(response, 0).put("cabinClass", "W");
+        offer(response, 1).put("cabinClass", "C");
+
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(response);
+
+        assertThat(itineraries).extracting(ItineraryDTO::getCabin).startsWith(CabinClass.PREMIUM_ECONOMY,
+                CabinClass.BUSINESS, CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#11 #24] an unknown cabin discards only that offer and logs a WARN")
+    void unknownCabinDiscardsTheOffer(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        offer(response, 0).put("cabinClass", "F");
+
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(response);
+
+        assertThat(itineraries).hasSize(4);
+        assertThat(ids(itineraries)).doesNotContain("d15896b6-d0f0-4808-a420-82ea5650421b");
+        assertThat(output).contains("The offer d15896b6-d0f0-4808-a420-82ea5650421b of the provider alpha can not be "
+                + "transformed: Unknown cabin class F");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#21 #24] without the fare of a requested passenger type the offer is discarded")
+    void missingFareDiscardsTheOffer(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        offer(response, 1).withObject("/pricing").withArray("passengers").remove(1);
+
+        assertThat(map(response)).hasSize(4);
+        assertThat(output).contains("can not be transformed: The provider does not return the fare for CHILD");
+        // Without children in the request the same offer is valid
+        assertThat(mapper.map(Fixtures.convert(response, AlphaAvailabilityResponse.class),
+                Requests.request("BUE,MIA", "MIA,BUE", "2027-01-14,2027-01-24", 1, 0, 1))).hasSize(5);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#24] an empty response or without results returns an empty list")
+    void emptyResponses() {
+        assertThat(mapper.map(null, Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(mapper.map(new AlphaAvailabilityResponse(null), Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+    }
+
+    private List<ItineraryDTO> map(ObjectNode response) {
+        return mapper.map(Fixtures.convert(response, AlphaAvailabilityResponse.class), Requests.roundTrip());
+    }
+
+    private ObjectNode offer(ObjectNode response, int index) {
+        return (ObjectNode) response.withArray("results").get(index);
+    }
+}

+
+import static com.twa.flights.support.ItineraryTestData.ids;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+

+
+        assertThat(ids(itineraries)).containsExactly("f8466941-3630-4df4-a495-c617a5fbde15",
+                "e432414e-a318-4e34-8cde-26ef07b2f041", "26797bdf-b072-4874-9eb7-1329e6715ad7",
+                "af182674-446e-4048-a3c7-8dd012920f96", "c3fdbd74-6e3b-4df2-b9c2-dbf7558f1fd0");
+
+        ItineraryDTO first = itineraries.get(0);
+        assertThat(first.getCarrier()).isEqualTo("LA");
+        assertThat(first.getProvider()).isEqualTo(Provider.BETA);
+        assertThat(first.getFlightType()).isEqualTo(FlightType.ROUND_TRIP);
+        assertThat(first.getCabin()).isEqualTo(CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(0).getLegs()).containsExactly(
+                new LegDTO("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", "08:30", "2027-01-14", "20:45", 1347, null));
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(1).getLegs()).containsExactly(
+                new LegDTO("MIA", "BUE", "2027-01-24", "05:45", "2027-01-24", "21:15", 4216, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12] the prices come in cents and they are divided by 100 with two decimals")
+    void pricesInCents() {
+        ItineraryDTO first = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP)).get(0);
+
+        // adult: base_fare_cents 10812 and tax_cents 13960
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getAdults().getBase()).hasToString("108.12");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getAdults().getTax()).hasToString("139.60");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getAdults().getSubtotal()).hasToString("247.72");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getChildren().getSubtotal()).hasToString("640.02");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getInfants().getSubtotal()).hasToString("322.58");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12 #22] the baggage comes from hand and checked, without pieces the weight is null")
+    void baggage() {
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP));
+
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(0).getBaggage().getCarryOn()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 8));
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(0).getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 23));
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(1).getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(0, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12] the OUTBOUND bound is always the first segment")
+    void outboundIsTheFirstSegment() {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        ArrayNode bounds = option(response, 0).withArray("bounds");
+        JsonNode outbound = bounds.remove(0);
+        bounds.add(outbound);
+        assertThat(bounds.get(0).get("direction").asText()).isEqualTo("INBOUND");
+
+        ItineraryDTO first = map(response).get(0);
+
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(0).firstLeg().getOrigin()).isEqualTo("BUE");
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(1).firstLeg().getOrigin()).isEqualTo("MIA");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12] the cabin is the name of the enum in any case, an unknown one discards the option")
+    void cabin(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        option(response, 0).put("cabin", "premium_economy");
+        option(response, 1).put("cabin", "Business");
+        option(response, 2).put("cabin", "first");
+
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(response);
+
+        assertThat(itineraries).hasSize(4);
+        assertThat(itineraries).extracting(ItineraryDTO::getCabin).startsWith(CabinClass.PREMIUM_ECONOMY,
+                CabinClass.BUSINESS, CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+        assertThat(output).contains(
+                "The option 26797bdf-b072-4874-9eb7-1329e6715ad7 of the provider beta can not be transformed");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12] with a status different from OK the response is ignored")
+    void statusDifferentFromOk(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        response.put("status", "ERROR");
+
+        assertThat(map(response)).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(output).contains("The provider beta returns the status ERROR");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#12] an empty response, without data or without options returns an empty list")
+    void emptyResponses() {
+        assertThat(mapper.map(null, Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(mapper.map(new BetaAvailabilityResponse("OK", null), Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(mapper.map(new BetaAvailabilityResponse("OK", new BetaAvailabilityResponse.Data(null)),
+                Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+    }
+
+    private List<ItineraryDTO> map(ObjectNode response) {
+        return mapper.map(Fixtures.convert(response, BetaAvailabilityResponse.class), Requests.roundTrip());
+    }
+
+    private ObjectNode option(ObjectNode response, int index) {
+        return (ObjectNode) response.withObject("/data").withArray("flight_options").get(index);
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.provider.mapper;
+
+import static com.twa.flights.support.ItineraryTestData.ids;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+import static org.assertj.core.groups.Tuple.tuple;
+
+import java.util.List;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;

+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import com.google.common.collect.Lists;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.BaggageAllowanceDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.ItineraryDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.LegDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.provider.dto.GammaAvailabilityResponse;
+import com.twa.flights.shared.enums.CabinClass;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.FlightType;
+import com.twa.flights.provider.api.Provider;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Fixtures;
+import com.twa.flights.support.ProviderStubs;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Requests;
+
+// Characterization of the transformation of the provider Gamma with its recorded response (behaviors #13 and #24)
+@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
+class GammaItineraryMapperTest {
+
+    private static final String JA_WITH_CONNECTIONS = "fa07a8be-bff5-4278-8ec9-0530ca982839";
+
+    private final GammaItineraryMapper mapper = new GammaItineraryMapper(new ProviderMapperHelper());
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#13] each itinerary of Gamma is an itinerary, the dates lose the seconds")
+    void recordedResponse() {
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.GAMMA_ROUND_TRIP));
+
+        assertThat(ids(itineraries)).containsExactly("3611ff29-66f5-4444-be3e-6e55539f1e9b",
+                "9c3d4c18-8e2f-401c-8b7d-5d8cf3677204", "df7f294f-7d88-4165-9625-5e67e7a395b7", JA_WITH_CONNECTIONS,
+                "df3a40aa-5aa7-442f-b816-41572f15796b");
+
+        ItineraryDTO first = itineraries.get(0);
+        assertThat(first.getCarrier()).isEqualTo("H2");
+        assertThat(first.getProvider()).isEqualTo(Provider.GAMMA);
+        assertThat(first.getFlightType()).isEqualTo(FlightType.ROUND_TRIP);
+        assertThat(first.getCabin()).isEqualTo(CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+        assertThat(first.getSegments().get(0).getLegs()).containsExactly(
+                new LegDTO("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", "07:15", "2027-01-14", "22:30", 7231, null));
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getAdults().getSubtotal()).hasToString("367.20");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getChildren().getSubtotal()).hasToString("530.62");
+        assertThat(first.getPriceInfo().getInfants().getSubtotal()).hasToString("191.40");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#13] the flights are grouped by SegmentIndex and sorted by Sequence, whatever the order of the list")
+    void flightsAreGroupedBySegmentIndex() {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.GAMMA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        ArrayNode flights = itinerary(response, 3).withArray("Flights");
+        List<JsonNode> reversed = Lists.reverse(Lists.newArrayList(flights.elements()));
+        flights.removeAll();
+        flights.addAll(reversed);
+
+        ItineraryDTO withConnections = map(response).get(3);
+
+        assertThat(withConnections.getSegments()).hasSize(2);
+        assertThat(withConnections.getSegments().get(0).getLegs())
+                .extracting(LegDTO::getOrigin, LegDTO::getDestination)
+                .containsExactly(tuple("BUE", "SAO"), tuple("SAO", "MIA"));
+        assertThat(withConnections.getSegments().get(1).getLegs())
+                .extracting(LegDTO::getOrigin, LegDTO::getDestination)
+                .containsExactly(tuple("MIA", "SAO"), tuple("SAO", "BUE"));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#13] the baggage is a text nPC[mKG], without pieces the weight is null")
+    void baggageAsText() {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.GAMMA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        itinerary(response, 2).withObject("/Bags").put("Checked", "2PC");
+
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(response);
+
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(0).getBaggage().getCarryOn()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 8));
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(0).getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 23));
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(1).getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(0, null));
+        assertThat(itineraries.get(2).getBaggage().getChecked()).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(2, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#13 #24] an unknown format of baggage discards only that itinerary and logs a WARN")
+    void unknownBaggageFormat(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.GAMMA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        itinerary(response, 0).withObject("/Bags").put("Checked", "23KG");
+        itinerary(response, 1).withObject("/Bags").put("CarryOn", "1pc8kg");
+
+        assertThat(map(response)).hasSize(3);
+        assertThat(output).contains("The itinerary 3611ff29-66f5-4444-be3e-6e55539f1e9b of the provider gamma can not "
+                + "be transformed: Unknown format of baggage 23KG");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#13] the cabin uses ECO, PEY and BUS, an unknown one discards the itinerary")
+    void cabin() {
+        ObjectNode response = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.GAMMA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        itinerary(response, 0).put("CabinType", "PEY");
+        itinerary(response, 1).put("CabinType", "BUS");
+        itinerary(response, 2).put("CabinType", "FST");
+
+        List<ItineraryDTO> itineraries = map(response);
+
+        assertThat(itineraries).hasSize(4);
+        assertThat(itineraries).extracting(ItineraryDTO::getCabin).startsWith(CabinClass.PREMIUM_ECONOMY,
+                CabinClass.BUSINESS, CabinClass.ECONOMY);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#24] an empty response or without itineraries returns an empty list")
+    void emptyResponses() {
+        assertThat(mapper.map(null, Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(mapper.map(new GammaAvailabilityResponse(null), Requests.roundTrip())).isEmpty();
+    }
+
+    private List<ItineraryDTO> map(ObjectNode response) {
+        return mapper.map(Fixtures.convert(response, GammaAvailabilityResponse.class), Requests.roundTrip());
+    }
+
+    private ObjectNode itinerary(ObjectNode response, int index) {
+        return (ObjectNode) response.withArray("Itineraries").get(index);
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.provider.mapper;
+
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
+
+import java.math.BigDecimal;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.BaggageAllowanceDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.LegDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.PriceInfoDTO;
+import com.twa.flights.search.dto.FlightType;
+import com.twa.flights.shared.enums.PassengerType;
+import com.twa.flights.provider.mapper.ProviderMapperHelper.Fare;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Requests;
+
+// Characterization of the common rules of the mappers of the providers (behaviors #21 and #22)
+class ProviderMapperHelperTest {
+
+    private final ProviderMapperHelper helper = new ProviderMapperHelper();
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#21] subtotal = (base + tax) x quantity and total = subtotal, without markup")
+    void priceByPassengerType() {
+        PriceInfoDTO priceInfo = helper.createPriceInfo(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 2, 1, 1),
+                fare("339.39", "25.72"), fare("100.00", "20.50"), fare("10.00", "1.00"));
+
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getType()).isEqualTo(PassengerType.ADULT);
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getQuantity()).isEqualTo(2);
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getBase()).hasToString("339.39");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getTax()).hasToString("25.72");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getSubtotal()).hasToString("730.22");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getTotal()).hasToString("730.22");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getAdults().getMarkup()).isNull();
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getChildren().getSubtotal()).hasToString("120.50");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getChildren().getType()).isEqualTo(PassengerType.CHILD);
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getInfants().getSubtotal()).hasToString("11.00");
+        assertThat(priceInfo.getInfants().getType()).isEqualTo(PassengerType.INFANT);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#21] children and infants only exist when the quantity is higher than zero")
+    void childrenAndInfantsOnlyWithQuantity() {
+        PriceInfoDTO withZero = helper.createPriceInfo(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 1, 0, 0),
+                fare("1", "1"), fare("1", "1"), fare("1", "1"));
+        PriceInfoDTO withNull = helper.createPriceInfo(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 1, null, null),
+                fare("1", "1"), null, null);
+
+        assertThat(withZero.getChildren()).isNull();
+        assertThat(withZero.getInfants()).isNull();
+        assertThat(withNull.getChildren()).isNull();
+        assertThat(withNull.getInfants()).isNull();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#21] without the fare of a requested passenger type the itinerary can not be created")
+    void missingFareOfRequestedPassenger() {
+        assertThatThrownBy(() -> helper.createPriceInfo(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 1, 1, 0),
+                fare("1", "1"), null, null)).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class)
+                .hasMessage("The provider does not return the fare for CHILD");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#22] the flight number keeps only the digits and the time is cut to HH:mm")
+    void legWithNumberAndTime() {
+        LegDTO leg = helper.createLeg("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14T07:15:00", "2027-01-14T22:30", "AR01234");
+
+        assertThat(leg.getOrigin()).isEqualTo("BUE");
+        assertThat(leg.getDestination()).isEqualTo("MIA");
+        assertThat(leg.getDepartureDate()).isEqualTo("2027-01-14");
+        assertThat(leg.getDepartureTime()).isEqualTo("07:15");
+        assertThat(leg.getArrivalDate()).isEqualTo("2027-01-14");
+        assertThat(leg.getArrivalTime()).isEqualTo("22:30");
+        assertThat(leg.getNumber()).isEqualTo(1234);
+        assertThat(leg.getFlightDuration()).isNull();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#22] a flight number without digits can not be transformed")
+    void flightNumberWithoutDigits() {
+        assertThatThrownBy(() -> helper.createLeg("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14T07:15", "2027-01-14T22:30", "AR"))
+                .isInstanceOf(NumberFormatException.class);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#22] without pieces of baggage the weight is null")
+    void allowanceWithoutPieces() {
+        assertThat(helper.createAllowance(1, 23)).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(1, 23));
+        assertThat(helper.createAllowance(0, 23)).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(0, null));
+        assertThat(helper.createAllowance(null, 23)).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(null, null));
+        assertThat(helper.createAllowance(2, null)).isEqualTo(new BaggageAllowanceDTO(2, null));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#22] the flight type is ROUND_TRIP when there is more than one destination, also for multiple destinations")
+    void flightType() {
+        assertThat(helper.getFlightType(Requests.request("BUE", "MIA", "2027-01-14", 1, 0, 0)))
+                .isEqualTo(FlightType.ONE_WAY);
+        assertThat(helper.getFlightType(Requests.request("BUE,MIA", "MIA,BUE", "2027-01-14,2027-01-24", 1, 0, 0)))
+                .isEqualTo(FlightType.ROUND_TRIP);
+        assertThat(helper.getFlightType(Requests.request("BUE,MIA,SCL", "MIA,SCL,BUE", "2027-01-14", 1, 0, 0)))
+                .isEqualTo(FlightType.ROUND_TRIP);
+    }
+
+    private static Fare fare(String base, String tax) {
+        return new Fare(new BigDecimal(base), new BigDecimal(tax));
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.provider.service;
+
+import static com.twa.flights.support.Json.findById;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Set;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
+import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
+import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import com.google.common.collect.Lists;
+import com.google.common.collect.Sets;
+import com.twa.flights.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Fixtures;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Json;
+import com.twa.flights.support.ProviderStubs;
+
+/**
+ * Characterization of the search with the providers through the public API: the failures of a provider, the duration
+ * of the flights with the catalog and the itineraries that are discarded (behaviors #10, #15 and #24).
+ */
+@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
+class ProvidersSearchTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {
+
+    private static final String ALPHA_AR = "adb49624-bf8c-4da6-904e-2892cba9f9b6";
+    private static final String ALPHA_AR_WITH_CONNECTION = "e485161b-e5e1-44fa-a786-3aaab579c6a6";
+    private static final String GAMMA_LA = "df3a40aa-5aa7-442f-b816-41572f15796b";
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#10] if a provider responds with an error the search continues with the others")
+    void providerWithError(CapturedOutput output) {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.ALPHA, 500);
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(providersOf(response)).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("BETA", "GAMMA");
+        assertThat(output).contains("Error while calling endpoint /itineraries with status code 500");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#10] if a provider takes more than 5 seconds the search continues without it")
+    void providerWithTimeout() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        providers.respondWithDelay(ProviderStubs.BETA, ProviderStubs.BETA_ROUND_TRIP, 6000);
+
+        long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+        long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(providersOf(response)).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("ALPHA", "GAMMA");
+        // The read timeout of the connector is 5 seconds
+        assertThat(elapsed).isBetween(5000L, 6000L);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#10] if a provider returns a body that is not JSON the search continues without it")
+    void providerWithInvalidBody() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        providers.respondWithBody(ProviderStubs.GAMMA, "this is not json");
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(providersOf(response)).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("ALPHA", "BETA");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#10] if the three providers fail the response is 200 without itineraries and the search is saved anyway")
+    void allProvidersFail() {
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.ALPHA, 500);
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.BETA, 503);
+        providers.respondWithStatus(ProviderStubs.GAMMA, 400);
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(response.getBody());
+        assertThat(body.get("itineraries")).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(body.get("pagination")).isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"offset\":0,\"limit\":0,\"total\":0}"));
+        awaitSearchSaved(body.get("id").asText());
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] the duration of each leg and segment uses the time zones of the cities of the catalog")
+    void durationOfTheFlights() {
+        providers.onlyAlphaReturns(Fixtures.read(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP));
+
+        JsonNode itineraries = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody()).get("itineraries");
+
+        // BUE 11:00 (UTC-3) to MIA 18:45 (UTC-5) and MIA 09:45 to BUE 21:45
+        JsonNode direct = findById(itineraries, ALPHA_AR);
+        assertThat(direct.at("/segments/0/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("09:45");
+        assertThat(direct.at("/segments/0/legs/0/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("09:45");
+        assertThat(direct.at("/segments/1/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("10:00");
+
+        // BUE 02:15 to SAO 08:30 and SAO 12:45 to MIA 23:00: the segment goes from the first departure to the last
+        // arrival, including the connection
+        JsonNode withConnection = findById(itineraries, ALPHA_AR_WITH_CONNECTION);
+        assertThat(withConnection.at("/segments/0/legs/0/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("06:15");
+        assertThat(withConnection.at("/segments/0/legs/1/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("12:15");
+        assertThat(withConnection.at("/segments/0/flightDuration").asText()).isEqualTo("22:45");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] an itinerary that arrives before its departure is discarded and an ERROR is logged")
+    void arrivalBeforeDepartureDiscardsTheItinerary(CapturedOutput output) {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        // The LA itinerary of Gamma: MIA 07:15 (UTC-5) to SAO 09:00 (UTC-3)
+        assertThat(response.getBody()).doesNotContain(GAMMA_LA);
+        assertThat(output).contains("Occur an error calculating the flight duration of id " + GAMMA_LA);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#15] an itinerary with a city that is not in the catalog is discarded and an ERROR is logged")
+    void cityOutsideTheCatalogDiscardsTheItinerary(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode alpha = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        ((ObjectNode) alpha.at("/results/1/journeys/0/flights/0")).put("to", "XYZ");
+        providers.onlyAlphaReturns(alpha.toString());
+
+        List<String> ids = idsOf(search(roundTripRequest()));
+
+        assertThat(ids).hasSize(4).doesNotContain(ALPHA_AR);
+        assertThat(output).contains("Occur an error calculating the flight duration of id " + ALPHA_AR);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#24] an offer that can not be transformed is discarded with a WARN, the search continues")
+    void malformedOfferIsDiscarded(CapturedOutput output) {
+        ObjectNode alpha = Fixtures.tree(ProviderStubs.ALPHA_ROUND_TRIP);
+        ((ObjectNode) alpha.at("/results/1")).put("cabinClass", "X");
+        providers.onlyAlphaReturns(alpha.toString());
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(idsOf(response)).hasSize(4).doesNotContain(ALPHA_AR);
+        assertThat(output).contains("The offer " + ALPHA_AR + " of the provider alpha can not be transformed");
+    }
+
+    private Set<String> providersOf(ResponseEntity<String> response) {
+        Set<String> providers = Sets.newHashSet();
+        Json.parse(response.getBody()).get("itineraries")
+                .forEach(itinerary -> providers.add(itinerary.get("provider").asText()));
+        return providers;
+    }
+
+    private List<String> idsOf(ResponseEntity<String> response) {
+        List<String> ids = Lists.newArrayList();
+        Json.parse(response.getBody()).get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> ids.add(itinerary.get("id").asText()));
+        return ids;
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.search.controller;
+
+import static com.twa.flights.support.Json.decimal;
+import static com.twa.flights.support.Json.findById;
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpEntity;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
+import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
+import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.google.common.collect.Lists;
+import com.twa.flights.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Json;
+
+/**
+ * Characterization of the prices that the search returns with the recorded responses of the three providers
+ * (behaviors #1, #3, #4, #5 and #9). The request has 1 adult, 1 child and 1 infant, like the recorded responses.
+ */
+class ClustersPricingTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {
+
+    // AR of the provider Alpha: ADT 322.62 + 64.67, CHD 95.70 + 123.26, INF 119.29 + 29.98
+    private static final String ALPHA_AR = "adb49624-bf8c-4da6-904e-2892cba9f9b6";
+    // Carriers without a commercial policy
+    private static final String BETA_LA = "f8466941-3630-4df4-a495-c617a5fbde15";
+    private static final String BETA_FY = "af182674-446e-4048-a3c7-8dd012920f96";
+
+    private static final String GENERAL_POLICY_AR = "/pricing/commercial-policies/1/carriers/AR";
+
+    @AfterEach
+    void restoreGeneralPolicy() {
+        changeGeneralPercentageOfAR("5");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#1 #9] the search returns the markup of each passenger type with the scale of BigDecimal, without rounding")
+    void pricesOfTheSearchAreNotRounded() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(roundTripRequest());
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        JsonNode priceInfo = findById(Json.parse(response.getBody()).get("itineraries"), ALPHA_AR).get("priceInfo");
+
+        assertPaxPrice(priceInfo.get("adults"), "322.62", "64.67", "387.29", "5.00", "193.6450", "580.9350");
+        assertPaxPrice(priceInfo.get("children"), "95.70", "123.26", "218.96", "5.00", "109.4800", "328.4400");
+        assertPaxPrice(priceInfo.get("infants"), "119.29", "29.98", "149.27", "5.00", "74.6350", "223.9050");
+        // The exact text of the JSON, a client that parses the numbers as text depends on it
+        assertThat(response.getBody()).contains("\"total\":580.9350", "\"percentage\":5.00");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#3] the itineraries of carriers without a policy (LA, FY) are not in the response")
+    void itinerariesWithoutPolicyAreNotReturned() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+
+        List<String> ids = Lists.newArrayList();
+        body.get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> ids.add(itinerary.get("id").asText()));
+        List<String> carriers = Lists.newArrayList();
+        body.get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> carriers.add(itinerary.get("carrier").asText()));
+
+        // 15 itineraries recorded: 5 Alpha, 5 Beta (LA and FY without policy) and 5 Gamma (the LA one is discarded
+        // before the pricing because of its duration, see ProvidersSearchTest)
+        assertThat(ids).hasSize(12).doesNotContain(BETA_LA, BETA_FY);
+        assertThat(carriers).doesNotContain("LA", "FY");
+        assertThat(body.at("/pagination/total").asInt()).isEqualTo(12);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#4] a change of a policy is used by the next search, the saved searches keep the old price")
+    void policyChangesAffectOnlyTheNewSearches() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode before = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+        String savedSearch = before.get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(savedSearch);
+
+        changeGeneralPercentageOfAR("9");
+
+        JsonNode after = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+        assertThat(decimal(findById(after.get("itineraries"), ALPHA_AR).at("/priceInfo/adults/markup/percentage")))
+                .isEqualTo("9.00");
+        assertThat(decimal(findById(after.get("itineraries"), ALPHA_AR).at("/priceInfo/adults/total")))
+                .isEqualTo("735.8510");
+
+        // The pagination of the first search uses the snapshot saved with the old policy
+        JsonNode page = Json.parse(
+                search(with(roundTripRequest(), "id", savedSearch, "offset", 0)).getBody());
+        assertThat(decimal(findById(page.get("itineraries"), ALPHA_AR).at("/priceInfo/adults/markup/percentage")))
+                .isEqualTo("5.00");
+        assertThat(decimal(findById(page.get("itineraries"), ALPHA_AR).at("/priceInfo/adults/total")))
+                .isEqualTo("580.9350");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#5] the search is public, it does not need the API key of the pricing")
+    void searchDoesNotNeedTheApiKey() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        assertThat(search(roundTripRequest()).getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    // private function(s)
+
+    private void assertPaxPrice(JsonNode paxPrice, String base, String tax, String subtotal, String percentage,
+            String markup, String total) {
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.get("base"))).isEqualTo(base);
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.get("tax"))).isEqualTo(tax);
+        assertThat(paxPrice.get("quantity").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.get("subtotal"))).isEqualTo(subtotal);
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.at("/markup/percentage"))).isEqualTo(percentage);
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.at("/markup/amount"))).isEqualTo(markup);
+        assertThat(decimal(paxPrice.get("total"))).isEqualTo(total);
+    }
+
+    private void changeGeneralPercentageOfAR(String percentage) {
+        HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();
+        headers.set("X-Api-Key", API_KEY);
+        headers.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = restTemplate.exchange(uri(GENERAL_POLICY_AR, Map.of()), HttpMethod.PUT,
+                new HttpEntity<>("{\"percentage\":" + percentage + "}", headers), String.class);
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.search.controller;
+
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+
+import java.time.Duration;
+import java.time.LocalDateTime;
+import java.util.List;

+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
+import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
+import org.springframework.web.util.UriUtils;

+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.google.common.collect.Lists;

+/**
+ * Characterization of the search through the public API with the recorded responses of the providers: the contract of
+ * the response, the saved search and the pagination (behaviors #8, #14, #16, #17, #27, #29, #30 and #31).
+ *
+ * With the recorded responses the search has 12 itineraries: 5 of Alpha, 3 of Beta and 4 of Gamma.
+ */
+class ClustersSearchTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

+    private static final String ITINERARIES = "/clusters/itineraries";
+
+    @Autowired
+    private ClusterSearchCleanupJob cleanupJob;
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#8] the response has id, pagination and itineraries with the attributes in lowerCamelCase")
+    void contractOfTheResponse() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+
+        assertThat(fields(body)).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("id", "pagination", "itineraries");
+        assertThat(fields(body.get("pagination"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("offset", "limit", "total");
+        JsonNode itinerary = body.get("itineraries").get(0);
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary)).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("id", "segments", "priceInfo", "provider",
+                "flightType", "carrier", "cabin", "baggage");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.at("/segments/0"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("legs", "flightDuration");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.at("/segments/0/legs/0"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("origin", "destination",
+                "departureDate", "departureTime", "arrivalDate", "arrivalTime", "number", "flightDuration");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.get("priceInfo"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("adults", "children", "infants");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.at("/priceInfo/adults"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("tax", "base", "quantity",
+                "subtotal", "total", "type", "markup");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.at("/priceInfo/adults/markup"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("percentage",
+                "amount");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.get("baggage"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("carryOn", "checked");
+        assertThat(fields(itinerary.at("/baggage/checked"))).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("pieces", "weightKg");
+        assertThat(itinerary.get("flightType").asText()).isEqualTo("ROUND_TRIP");
+        assertThat(itinerary.at("/priceInfo/adults/type").asText()).isEqualTo("ADULT");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#8] without children or infants their prices are null in the JSON")
+    void pricesOfPassengersThatAreNotRequested() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode itinerary = Json.parse(search(with(roundTripRequest(), "children", 0, "infants", 0)).getBody())
+                .get("itineraries").get(0);
+
+        assertThat(itinerary.at("/priceInfo/children").isNull()).isTrue();
+        assertThat(itinerary.at("/priceInfo/infants").isNull()).isTrue();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14] the id has the timestamp and the parameters of the request")
+    void idOfTheSearch() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        String id = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody()).get("id").asText();
+
+        assertThat(id).matches("TWA_g=\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+_f=BUE,MIA_t=MIA,BUE_d=" + roundTripDates()
+                + "_a=1_c=1_i=1_cb=ECONOMY");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#16] without amount all the itineraries are in one page: limit = total")
+    void withoutAmount() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+
+        assertThat(body.get("pagination")).isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"offset\":0,\"limit\":12,\"total\":12}"));
+        assertThat(body.get("itineraries")).hasSize(12);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#16] the amount is applied to each provider: amount=2 asks 2 to each one and the total is 5, not 2")
+    void amountByProvider() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(search(with(roundTripRequest(), "amount", 2)).getBody());
+
+        // Alpha 2 (AA, AR), Beta 1 (the LA option has no policy) and Gamma 2 (H2, H2)
+        assertThat(body.get("pagination")).isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"offset\":0,\"limit\":2,\"total\":5}"));
+        assertThat(body.get("itineraries")).hasSize(2);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#17 #20] the offset is the number of the page and the pages keep the order by price")
+    void pagination() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        Map<String, Object> request = with(roundTripRequest(), "amount", 2);
+        JsonNode first = Json.parse(search(request).getBody());
+        String id = first.get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        JsonNode page0 = Json.parse(search(with(request, "id", id, "offset", 0)).getBody());
+        JsonNode page1 = Json.parse(search(with(request, "id", id, "offset", 1)).getBody());
+        JsonNode page2 = Json.parse(search(with(request, "id", id, "offset", 2)).getBody());
+        JsonNode page3 = Json.parse(search(with(request, "id", id, "offset", 3)).getBody());
+
+        assertThat(ids(page0)).isEqualTo(ids(first));
+        assertThat(page1.get("pagination")).isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"offset\":1,\"limit\":2,\"total\":5}"));
+        assertThat(page1.get("id").asText()).isEqualTo(id);
+        assertThat(ids(page1)).hasSize(2);
+        assertThat(ids(page2)).hasSize(1);
+        assertThat(ids(page3)).isEmpty();
+        assertThat(page3.get("pagination")).isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"offset\":3,\"limit\":2,\"total\":5}"));
+
+        List<String> all = Lists.newArrayList(ids(page0));
+        all.addAll(ids(page1));
+        all.addAll(ids(page2));
+        assertThat(Sets.newHashSet(all)).hasSize(5);
+        assertThat(totals(page0, page1, page2)).isSorted();
+        // The providers are not called again
+        assertThat(providers.requests("/alpha")).hasSize(1);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#17 #19] each page applies again the filters and the order, total is the amount after the filters")
+    void filtersInThePagination() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        JsonNode first = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+        String id = first.get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        JsonNode onlyAR = Json.parse(search(with(roundTripRequest(), "id", id, "offset", 0, "carriers", "AR",
+                "sortBy", "PRICE", "sortOrder", "DESC")).getBody());
+
+        // AR: 2 of Alpha and 3 of Beta
+        assertThat(onlyAR.at("/pagination/total").asInt()).isEqualTo(5);
+        assertThat(onlyAR.at("/pagination/limit").asInt()).isEqualTo(5);
+        Set<String> carriers = Sets.newHashSet();
+        onlyAR.get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> carriers.add(itinerary.get("carrier").asText()));
+        assertThat(carriers).containsExactly("AR");
+        assertThat(Lists.reverse(totals(onlyAR))).isSorted();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#31] with an id the result comes from the saved search, even if the other parameters are different")
+    void paginationIgnoresTheParameters() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        String id = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody()).get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        JsonNode other = Json.parse(search(with(roundTripRequest(), "from", "SCL", "to", "LIM", "departure",
+                departureDate(), "id", id, "offset", 0)).getBody());
+
+        assertThat(other.at("/pagination/total").asInt()).isEqualTo(12);
+        assertThat(other.at("/itineraries/0/segments/0/legs/0/origin").asText()).isEqualTo("BUE");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#30] with an id and without offset the response is 500")
+    void paginationWithoutOffset() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        String id = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody()).get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        assertThat(search(with(roundTripRequest(), "id", id)).getStatusCode())
+                .isEqualTo(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14 #7] the pagination of an unknown search responds 502 with the code 40015 and without description")
+    void paginationOfUnknownSearch() {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(with(roundTripRequest(), "id", "TWA_unknown", "offset", 0));
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(response.getBody()))
+                .isEqualTo(Json.parse("{\"code\":40015,\"shortDescription\":null,\"reasons\":null}"));
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14] an expired search can not be paginated (502) and the job removes it")
+    void expiredSearch() {
+        String expired = saveSearch("TWA_expired", LocalDateTime.now().minusMinutes(1));
+        String valid = saveSearch("TWA_valid", LocalDateTime.now().plusMinutes(5));
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(with(roundTripRequest(), "id", expired, "offset", 0));
+        ResponseEntity<String> itinerary = get("/clusters/" + expired + "/itineraries/ar", Map.of(), String.class);
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
+        assertThat(itinerary.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
+
+        // [#29] the cleanup job removes only the expired searches
+        cleanupJob.removeExpiredSearches();
+        assertThat(clusterSearchJpaRepository.existsById(expired)).isFalse();
+        assertThat(clusterSearchJpaRepository.existsById(valid)).isTrue();
+        clusterSearchJpaRepository.deleteById(valid);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14 #27] the search is saved in snake_case with a TTL of 5 minutes")
+    void savedSearch() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        String id = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody()).get("id").asText();
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        ClusterSearch saved = clusterSearchJpaRepository.findById(id).orElseThrow();
+
+        assertThat(Duration.between(saved.getCreatedAt(), saved.getExpireAt())).isEqualTo(Duration.ofMinutes(5));
+        assertThat(saved.getPayload()).contains("\"price_info\"", "\"flight_type\"", "\"weight_kg\"")
+                .doesNotContain("\"priceInfo\"");
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14] GET /clusters/{id}/itineraries/{itineraryId} returns the itinerary of the saved search")
+    void itineraryOfTheSearch() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(search(roundTripRequest()).getBody());
+        String id = body.get("id").asText();
+        JsonNode itinerary = body.get("itineraries").get(0);
+        awaitSearchSaved(id);
+
+        // The id has commas, colons and equals: it has to be encoded in the path
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(java.net.URI.create(restTemplate.getRootUri())
+                .resolve(CONTEXT_PATH + "/clusters/" + UriUtils.encodePathSegment(id, "UTF-8") + "/itineraries/"
+                        + itinerary.get("id").asText()), String.class);
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(response.getBody())).isEqualTo(itinerary);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#14 #7] an unknown search responds 404 40401 and an unknown itinerary 404 40402")
+    void unknownSearchOrItinerary() {
+        String id = saveSearch("TWA_search", LocalDateTime.now().plusMinutes(5));
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> unknownSearch = get("/clusters/unknown/itineraries/ar", Map.of(), String.class);
+        ResponseEntity<String> unknownItinerary = get("/clusters/" + id + "/itineraries/unknown", Map.of(),
+                String.class);
+        ResponseEntity<String> known = get("/clusters/" + id + "/itineraries/ar", Map.of(), String.class);
+
+        assertThat(unknownSearch.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(unknownSearch.getBody())).isEqualTo(Json.parse(
+                "{\"code\":40401,\"shortDescription\":\"The search does not exist or it is expired\",\"reasons\":null}"));
+        assertThat(unknownItinerary.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(unknownItinerary.getBody())).isEqualTo(Json.parse(
+                "{\"code\":40402,\"shortDescription\":\"The itinerary does not exist in the search\",\"reasons\":null}"));
+        assertThat(known.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        clusterSearchJpaRepository.deleteById(id);
+    }
+
+    // private function(s)
+
+    private String saveSearch(String id, LocalDateTime expireAt) {
+        ClusterSearch search = new ClusterSearch();
+        search.setId(id);
+        search.setPayload("{\"id\":\"" + id + "\",\"pagination\":{\"offset\":0,\"limit\":1,\"total\":1},"
+                + "\"itineraries\":[{\"id\":\"ar\",\"carrier\":\"AR\",\"segments\":[],\"price_info\":null}]}");
+        search.setCreatedAt(expireAt.minusMinutes(5));
+        search.setExpireAt(expireAt);
+        clusterSearchJpaRepository.saveAndFlush(search);
+        return id;
+    }
+
+    private static Set<String> fields(JsonNode node) {
+        Set<String> fields = Sets.newHashSet();
+        node.fieldNames().forEachRemaining(fields::add);
+        return fields;
+    }
+
+    private static List<String> ids(JsonNode page) {
+        List<String> ids = Lists.newArrayList();
+        page.get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> ids.add(itinerary.get("id").asText()));
+        return ids;
+    }
+
+    private static List<java.math.BigDecimal> totals(JsonNode... pages) {
+        List<java.math.BigDecimal> totals = Lists.newArrayList();
+        for (JsonNode page : pages) {
+            page.get("itineraries").forEach(itinerary -> {
+                JsonNode priceInfo = itinerary.get("priceInfo");
+                java.math.BigDecimal total = java.math.BigDecimal.ZERO;
+                for (String type : List.of("adults", "children", "infants")) {
+                    if (!priceInfo.get(type).isNull()) {
+                        total = total.add(priceInfo.get(type).get("total").decimalValue());
+                    }
+                }
+                totals.add(total);
+            });
+        }
+        return totals;
+    }
+}

+package com.twa.flights.search.controller;
+
+import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
+
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.stream.Stream;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
+import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;
+import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.MethodSource;
+import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
+import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.twa.flights.enums.ExceptionStatus;
+import com.twa.flights.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
+import com.twa.flights.support.Json;
+
+/**
+ * Characterization of the validation of the search through the public API (behaviors #6, #7, #26, #30 and #32). The
+ * dates are relative to today because the validator uses LocalDate.now().
+ */
+class ClustersValidationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {
+
+    private static Map<String, Object> valid() {
+        Map<String, Object> params = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        params.put("from", "BUE");
+        params.put("to", "MIA");
+        params.put("departure", departureDate());
+        params.put("adults", 1);
+        params.put("children", 0);
+        params.put("infants", 0);
+        return params;
+    }
+
+    static Stream<Arguments> invalidRequests() {
+        String yesterday = LocalDate.now().minusDays(1).toString();
+        return Stream.of(
+                // Dates
+                Arguments.of("without departure", with(valid(), "departure", null), 40003),
+                Arguments.of("departure with another format", with(valid(), "departure", "14/01/2027"), 40007),
+                Arguments.of("departure in the past", with(valid(), "departure", "2020-01-01"), 40009),
+                Arguments.of("departure yesterday", with(valid(), "departure", yesterday), 40009),
+                Arguments.of("dates in the wrong order",
+                        with(valid(), "from", "BUE,MIA", "to", "MIA,BUE", "departure", returnDate() + ","
+                                + departureDate()), 40008),
+                // Passengers
+                Arguments.of("zero adults", with(valid(), "adults", 0), 40004),
+                Arguments.of("negative children", with(valid(), "children", -1), 40005),
+                Arguments.of("negative infants", with(valid(), "infants", -1), 40006),
+                Arguments.of("more than 9 adults and children", with(valid(), "adults", 5, "children", 5), 40010),
+                // Origins and destinations
+                Arguments.of("without origin", with(valid(), "from", null), 40001),
+                Arguments.of("empty origin", with(valid(), "from", ""), 40001),
+                Arguments.of("without destination", with(valid(), "to", null), 40002),
+                Arguments.of("code of four characters", with(valid(), "from", "BUEN"), 40011),
+                Arguments.of("code outside the catalog", with(valid(), "from", "XXX"), 40014),
+                Arguments.of("code of two characters outside the catalog", with(valid(), "to", "MI"), 40014),
+                Arguments.of("same origin and destination", with(valid(), "to", "BUE"), 40012),
+                Arguments.of("duplicated routes",
+                        with(valid(), "from", "BUE,BUE", "to", "MIA,MIA", "departure", departureDate() + ","
+                                + returnDate()), 40013),
+                // Sort, cabin and filters
+                Arguments.of("unknown sort criteria", with(valid(), "sortBy", "NAME"), 40016),
+                Arguments.of("unknown sort order", with(valid(), "sortOrder", "UP"), 40017),
+                Arguments.of("unknown cabin", with(valid(), "cabin", "FIRST"), 40018),
+                Arguments.of("negative maxStops", with(valid(), "maxStops", -1), 40019),
+                Arguments.of("negative minPrice", with(valid(), "minPrice", -1), 40020),
+                Arguments.of("minPrice higher than maxPrice", with(valid(), "minPrice", 10, "maxPrice", 5), 40020),
+                Arguments.of("carrier of three characters", with(valid(), "carriers", "ARG"), 40021),
+                Arguments.of("carriers without codes", with(valid(), "carriers", ","), 40021),
+                Arguments.of("excluded carrier of one character", with(valid(), "excludedCarriers", "A"), 40021),
+                Arguments.of("unknown departure time", with(valid(), "departureTime", "DAWN"), 40022));
+    }
+
+    @ParameterizedTest(name = "[#6 #7] {0} -> {2}")
+    @MethodSource("invalidRequests")
+    void invalidRequest(String description, Map<String, Object> params, int code) {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(params);
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(response.getBody())).isEqualTo(error(code));
+    }
+
+    static Stream<Arguments> severalErrors() {
+        return Stream.of(
+                Arguments.of("date in the past and zero adults",
+                        with(valid(), "departure", "2020-01-01", "adults", 0), 40009),
+                Arguments.of("zero adults and without origin", with(valid(), "adults", 0, "from", null), 40004),
+                Arguments.of("origin outside the catalog and same origin and destination",
+                        with(valid(), "from", "XXX", "to", "XXX"), 40014),
+                Arguments.of("origin outside the catalog and unknown sort", with(valid(), "from", "XXX", "sortBy",
+                        "NAME"), 40014),
+                Arguments.of("unknown sort and unknown cabin", with(valid(), "sortBy", "NAME", "cabin", "FIRST"),
+                        40016),
+                Arguments.of("unknown cabin and negative maxStops", with(valid(), "cabin", "FIRST", "maxStops", -1),
+                        40018));
+    }
+
+    @ParameterizedTest(name = "[#6] the first error wins: {0} -> {2}")
+    @MethodSource("severalErrors")
+    void firstErrorWins(String description, Map<String, Object> params, int code) {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(params);
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);
+        assertThat(Json.parse(response.getBody()).get("code").asInt()).isEqualTo(code);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#6] the enums accept lower case and spaces")
+    void enumsInAnyCase() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(with(valid(), "sortBy", " duration ", "sortOrder", "desc", "cabin",
+                "business", "departureTime", " Morning "));
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#6] a search for today is valid")
+    void searchForToday() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "departure", LocalDate.now().toString())).getStatusCode())
+                .isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#26] the codes of the cities are found in any case (collation of MySQL)")
+    void cityCodeInLowerCase() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "from", "bue", "to", "mia")).getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#32] the infants do not count in the maximum of 9 and they do not need an adult each")
+    void infantsWithoutLimit() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "adults", 9, "infants", 9)).getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "adults", 1, "infants", 5)).getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#30] without children or infants the response is 500 (unboxing of null), not a 400")
+    void withoutChildrenOrInfants() {
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "children", null)).getStatusCode())
+                .isEqualTo(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "infants", null)).getStatusCode())
+                .isEqualTo(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#30] with more origins than destinations the response is 500 (IndexOutOfBoundsException)")
+    void moreOriginsThanDestinations() {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(with(valid(), "from", "BUE,MIA", "to", "MIA", "departure",
+                departureDate() + "," + returnDate()));
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
+        // The default error of Spring Boot, not the ErrorDTO of the application
+        JsonNode body = Json.parse(response.getBody());
+        assertThat(body.get("status").asInt()).isEqualTo(500);
+        assertThat(body.has("code")).isFalse();
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#30] with more destinations than origins only the routes of the origins are validated")
+    void moreDestinationsThanOrigins() {
+        providers.allReturnRecordedRoundTrip();
+
+        assertThat(search(with(valid(), "from", "BUE", "to", "MIA,BUE")).getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.OK);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    @DisplayName("[#6] a parameter with a wrong type is a 400 of Spring, without the ErrorDTO")
+    void parameterWithWrongType() {
+        ResponseEntity<String> response = search(with(valid(), "adults", "one"));
+
+        assertThat(response.getStatusCode()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);
+        assertThat(response.getBody() == null || !response.getBody().contains("\"code\"")).isTrue();
+    }
+
+    private static JsonNode error(int code) {
+        ExceptionStatus status = java.util.Arrays.stream(ExceptionStatus.values())
+                .filter(value -> value.getCode() == code).findFirst().orElseThrow();
+        return Json.parse("{\"code\":" + code + ",\"shortDescription\":\"" + status.getMessage()
+                + "\",\"reasons\":null}");
+    }
+}
